# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-11-20 through 2026-11-26  
**Updated:** 2026-11-20T10:53:41+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-03-20

## Best date plans

### 2026-11-21 — Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi + Ju Percussion

**Dinner, then Ju Percussion.** New York-inspired Afro-Caribbean and American at Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. Ju Percussion starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+inside+David+Geffen+Hall%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&destination=1887+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.lincolncenter.org/series/lincoln-center-presents/ju-percussion-612) · [Event booking](https://tickets.lincolncenter.org/en/booking/production/33633) · [Restaurant](https://www.tatiananyc.com/)

### 2026-11-21 — Danish Christmas Fair + Cafe Paulette

**Danish Christmas Fair, then a meal.** French bistro at Cafe Paulette. It is a 24-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=102+Willow+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201%3B+food+at+66+Hicks+Street&destination=1+South+Elliott+Place%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.dskny.org/christmas-fair) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafepaulette.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/cafe-paulette)

### 2026-11-22 — Brooklyn Holiday Makers Market — Sunday + Lele's Roman

**Brooklyn Holiday Makers Market — Sunday, then a meal.** Roman Italian at Lele's Roman. It is 2.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=33+35th+Street%2C+Second+Floor%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11232&destination=252+Schermerhorn+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.marketsformakers.com/brooklyn/) · [Event booking](https://tickets.marketsformakers.com/2026-23-brooklyn-holiday-market-for-makers/) · [Restaurant](https://www.lelesroman.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/leles-roman)

### 2026-11-21 — Lele's Roman + Choir Social Club

**Dinner, then Choir Social Club.** Roman Italian at Lele's Roman. It is a 22-minute walk from the venue. Choir Social Club starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=252+Schermerhorn+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=168+7th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.ibeambrooklyn.com/home/2026/11/21/choir-social-club) · [Restaurant](https://www.lelesroman.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/leles-roman)

### 2026-11-24 — Grand Central Terminal Architecture Tour + Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi

**Grand Central Terminal Architecture Tour, then a meal.** New York-inspired Afro-Caribbean and American at Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12:30 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=89+East+42nd+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10017&destination=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+inside+David+Geffen+Hall%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.mas.org/events/grand-central-terminal-11-24-2026/) · [Event booking](https://secure.mas.org/forms/grand-central-terminal-registration-26) · [Restaurant](https://www.tatiananyc.com/)

### 2026-11-26 — NYC Resistor Public Craft Night + Cafe Paulette

## What to do this week

#### [Lightscape: Opening-Night Value Tickets](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape)

Time to confirm · outdoors · Adult timed entry $29; anytime entry $59; fees included · Brooklyn Botanic Garden · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 68.0  
The illuminated garden trail opens with a new route through trees, water features, and light installations. The official calendar identifies November 20 as a value night.
**Timing:** Evening timed entry in 15-minute windows; individual available entry times were not exposed.
**Practical note:** Enter only at 990 Washington Avenue. The approximately mile-long route is outdoors and wheelchair accessible; severe weather can cancel.
**Why now:** Opening night is also a lower-priced value night.
[Official](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape) · [Tickets / booking](https://events.leapevents.com/tickets/lightscape2026/listing/) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://theskint.com/fri-mon-11-20-23-skint-weekend/)

7:30 PM · theater · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 64.0

**Timing:** 80 minutes without intermission.

35mm Import. ## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 20, 2026: weekday lunches, happy hours, recent openings, Thanksgiving meals and a Saturday Danish food fair. Reported-only offers and unconfirmed holiday availability are identified.

Dining research: 2026-11-20T10:38:11+00:00

No Michelin award claims are added. Gui's website displays a 2025 Michelin Selected Restaurant badge, not a star designation. Its linked Michelin listing returned no readable content, so current recognition could not be independently verified. Guide inclusion must not be presented as a Michelin star.

### Bar Primi Penn District's $39 three-course weekday lunch

Deals & happy hours — Bar Primi Penn District

A fixed-price lunch with vegetable piccolini, a choice of pasta, chicken Caesar or meatball parm hero, and gelato or sorbet.

Price: $39 for three courses  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, 11:30am–3pm  
Conditions: Selected prix-fixe dishes only. Reservation requirement, additional minimum purchases, tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated. No expiration or holiday exclusions were found; holiday service is unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.barprimi.com/penn-district-menus/)

### Junoon's two- or three-course Lunch Prestige

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

Lunch includes basmati rice and naan, with vegetarian choices alongside chicken, seafood and goat dishes.

Price: Two courses $43; three courses $49  
Schedule: Lunch; exact participating days and hours unknown  
Conditions: 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Other tax and gratuity treatment, reservation requirements and additional minimum purchases are not stated. No offer end date was stated. Holiday availability is unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Amélie West Village's $26 weekday lunch and daily happy hour

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

Two-course weekday lunch offers a relatively affordable sit-down option. Later, happy hour includes oysters, small plates and a burger or mussels meal with a drink.

Price: Two-course lunch $26; lunch wine add-on $10. Happy hour: oysters $1.50 each; six oysters with a glass of wine $19; small plates $10; wine flights $14; burger and fries or moules frites with beer or wine $26  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Friday noon–3:30pm. Happy-hour food daily 4–6pm; happy-hour drinks daily from opening until 6pm  
Conditions: Lunch excludes tax and gratuity. Lunch supplements include burger +$5, steak or Niçoise salad +$8, truffle-fries substitution +$5; extra bread basket $3. Happy-hour tax, gratuity, service charges, minimum oyster order and reservation requirements are not stated. No expiration stated; holiday availability unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/bistro)

### The Mermaid Inn Chelsea's $2 shellfish happy hour

Deals & happy hours — The Mermaid Inn Chelsea

Chef's-choice oysters and littleneck clams anchor a happy hour that also includes mini chowder, fish tacos and discounted drinks.

Price: Oysters and littleneck clams $2 each, minimum six; mini chowder $4; mini fish taco $4 each; cocktails $12; wine $10; beer $8  
Schedule: 4:30–6pm; participating days not explicitly stated on the menu  
Conditions: The $2 price applies to chef's-choice oysters and littleneck clams, with a minimum order of six. Participating days, seating restrictions, reservation requirements, tax, gratuity and service charges are not specified in the happy-hour section. No expiration is stated; holiday participation is unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.themermaidnyc.com/the-mermaid-inn-chelsea-menus/)

### Nobody Told Me UWS: $5 snacks and $2 oysters, daily 3–6pm

Deals & happy hours — Nobody Told Me UWS

Happy-hour snacks include masala chickpeas, spicy chicken sliders, creole shrimp sliders and crispy fish tacos.

Price: Listed snacks $5; oysters $2 each, minimum four; draft beer $7; wine $10; selected cocktails $12  
Schedule: Daily, 3–6pm  
Conditions: Minimum four oysters. Drink prices apply to listed selections. Other minimum purchases, reservation requirements, seating restrictions, tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated. No expiration stated; holiday availability unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.nobodytoldme.nyc/menu/uws-happy-hour/)

### Charlies Bar & Kitchen's Monday–Wednesday specials

Deals & happy hours — Charlies Bar & Kitchen

#### [Nirvanna the Band the Show the Movie the Commentary Track — remaining listed screening](https://lowcinema.com/movie/nirvanna-the-band-the-show-the-movie-the-commentar/)

2026-12-02 · 6:00 PM · film · Low Cinema · Ridgewood · Queens · Score 68.0  
Matt Johnson's time-travel comedy screens with a commentary track by Johnson, Jay McCarrol, Jared Raab and Matt Greyson. This is a recorded commentary presentation, not an announced live appearance.
**Timing:** Screening starts at 6pm; film runtime 95 minutes.
**Practical note:** All sales are final, with no refunds or exchanges. Unused tickets are released to standby five minutes after the listed showtime.
**Why now:** There is demonstrated demand across the November 30–December 4 engagement, rather than a speculative sellout warning.
**Hot booking alert:** The official film page marks five of six listed screenings sold out. December 2 at 6pm is the only listed screening retaining a purchase link; exact remaining seat count is unknown.
